                                          CHAPTER 5

                     FIELD MAINTENANCE INSTRUCTIONS

    Note. This chapter contains information for field maintenance. The amount of repair that can be performed
by units having field maintenance responsibility is limited only by the tools and test equipment available and by
the skill of the repairman.
